large impact on the european side of things, especially now that several european nations
are coughing up classified files and bringing this stuff up to the public awareness level.

There have been qualified people saying this sort of thing for decades now, so in a sense
this is old news, but the context is promising and this could be a very big push forward to
one of the major nations puting disclosure on the table.

First it was barely hearable, then it was a whisper, then it was said out loud, now it was a
kerrang.

Things may just be changeing. Everywhere I look these days, from alternative energy to
UFO's, passing the Rothchilds and 911 there is an ever growing awareness, which I
personally think is the information age raising the group consciousness of mankind up a
few notches. I expect a lot of change over the next decade, most of it good, possibly
some bad. Either way it's a good time to be alive.

A few predictions... and I REALLY HOPE I'M WRONG!

1/There will be absolutely no major press coverage of this tomorrow... if there is it will be
consigned to a small paragraph in the middle of the newspaper, or the last article on the
news.... you know, the jokey 'my cat knows how to surf' spot

2/If there's even a whiff of more than just a few conspiracy nuts taking this seriously
there will be an effort from certain quarters to deflect attention from what's been said,
perhaps by officials 'clucking' in sympathy at how a once great man and American hero has
now gone slightly senile, or is suffering from some illness, or exhaustion, or anything that
hints at 'He's gone cuckoo!'.

3/Regardless, most people will blink, say 'Oh!' and go back to reading about Britney's latest
breakdown, or what colour undies Obama wears!

4/ I will bury my head in my hands (like I do most days) and despair at the rampant
shallowness and general ignorance that surrounds me!

Good predictions.

Here's another.

We will never have definitive and incontrovertible proof of a non-human non-terrestrial
alien coming to Earth, in the form of a live body, or clear genetic proof...I'll fudge and say
in our lifetimes.

They can't come here, they wouldn't come here and land even if they could, and they won't
be able to find us (rare accidental find notwithstanding).

I'll give you that seeding by biological precursors might possibly have occured, but it's
unlikely.

We may see a robot nano probe in the next several hundred years.

I'll also predict that humans as we know them, will never go beyond Low Earth Orbit
routinely, with perhaps occasional excursions to the Moon, but we'll never establish a
permanent base there.
permanent base there.

We'll never go to Mars, but un-Manned missions might happen with more robust robotic
probes - however I even give this lower probabiliy than certainty.

I'll also stipulate there are at least a handful of sentient, but planet-bound civs in our
Galaxy, but no Type II.

There are no Type III civilizations anywhere in the known Universe, IMO. There may have
been a couple at some time but they died out.

YMMV, but that's my take.

     Originally posted by Religion
     Of course we are not alone in the universe. Anyone who thinks so is self-centered and

That comment (not you) is a completely inept and rather clumsy way to put it.

There's no 'arrogance' involved, and no self-centeredness involved in a science-based
evaluation.

In addition, if by 'not alone' you mean other carbon-based life, OF COURSE there are other
carbon-based life examples, probably in our own solar system.

Why do I say an inept way of putting it, and by that I mean 'backwards' as in not
expository? It's argument by intimidation. "You disagree and we'll hate you or call your
estimate a crude name and threaten to expose your mother footware".

Virtual 100% certainty there is other carbon based life in our solar system. We know there
are Earth microbes on the Moon. (grin).

Virtual 100% certainty there are sentient civs in our Galaxy.

So why go into an argument based on intimidation? It's not necessary and gets us nowhere.


But if by 'arrogant' to say "we're alone" you mean that your comment implies aliens are
here or are coming here, then that's not accurate either. They aren't here because it's too
difficult to overcome the harsh conditions and vast distances, not because I think I'm
special.

Let's talk science and not devolve into emotion-based arguments.

Disclosure

There will never be 'Official Disclosure'. By that I mean a Press conference by the
President. The Pres may give veiled hints of something like Regan has done, but that's just
a way of putting an emotional point on a speech.

Why? There's no benefit in it for the gubmint.

If aliens ever come here en masse, we'll know it, but it's also possible that a staged event
could occur with specific control purposes.

2 cents.

reply to post by jamesder

Come on jamesder. Your pulling quotes from Wiki? That is not a credible source. Anyone
can post crap on Wiki. Is amazing how many people think what they read on Wikipedia is
the absolute truth.

Wikipedia.com unique is the implementation of software called "wiki wiki."
Wikipedia.com unique is the implementation of software called "wiki wiki."

"Wiki wiki" is a Hawaiian term that essentially means "fast fast" and refers to the fact that
instead of having a Web site that gives information on one particular topic, people should
have the ability to go on the Web site and add their own information by editing the actual
page. By doing this, people can add their own information with other people, but this is
also one of the reasons that Wikipedia.com should not necessarily be considered a credible
source.

So there is nothing preventing people that want to discredit Edgar Mitchell from posting
crap about him on Wiki. It happens all the time.

airteck
posted on Jul, 23 2008 @ 09:47 AM                                                                 link

Link
Here's the story published to sum it up for those who can't listen.


     

     Apollo 14 astronaut claims aliens HAVE made contact - but it has been covered up for 60
     years


     Aliens have contacted humans several times but governments have hidden the truth for
     60 years, the sixth man to walk on the moon has claimed.
     Apollo 14 astronaut Dr Edgar Mitchell, said he was aware of many UFO visits to Earth
     during his career with NASA but each one was covered up.

     Dr Mitchell, 77, said during a radio interview that sources at the space agency who had
     had contact with aliens described the beings as 'little people who look strange to us.'

     He said supposedly real-life ET's were similar to the traditional image of a small frame,
     large eyes and head.

     Chillingly, he claimed our technology is 'not nearly as sophisticated' as theirs and "had
     they been hostile", he warned 'we would be been gone by now'.

     Dr Mitchell, along with with Apollo 14 commander Alan Shepard, holds the record for the
     longest ever moon walk, at nine hours and 17 minutes following their 1971 mission.

     'I happen to have been privileged enough to be in on the fact that we've been visited on
     this planet and the UFO phenomena is real,' Dr Mitchell said.

     'It's been well covered up by all our governments for the last 60 years or so, but slowly
     it's leaked out and some of us have been privileged to have been briefed on some of it.


     UFO theorists believe Roswell in New Mexico was the site of an alien crash in 1947


     'I've been in military and intelligence circles, who know that beneath the surface of
     what has been public knowledge, yes - we have been visited. Reading the papers
     what has been public knowledge, yes - we have been visited. Reading the papers
     recently, it's been happening quite a bit.'

     Dr Mitchell, who has a Bachelor of Science degree in aeronautical engineering and a
     Doctor of Science degree in Aeronautics and Astronautics claimed Roswell was real and
     similar alien visits continue to be investigated.

     He told the astonished Kerrang! radio host Nick Margerrison: "This is really starting to
     open up. I think we're headed for real disclosure and some serious organisations are
     moving in that direction.'

     Mr Margerrison said: 'I thought I'd stumbled on some sort of astronaut humour but he
     was absolutely serious that aliens are definitely out there and there's no debating it.'

     Officials from NASA, however, were quick to play the comments down.


     In a statement, a spokesman said: "NASA does not track UFOs. NASA is not involved in
     any sort of cover up about alien life on this planet or anywhere in the universe.

     'Dr Mitchell is a great American, but we do not share his opinions on this issue.'

close minded about psychic abilities. Just because there has been no 'publicly presented'
proof of psychic abilities doesn't they are not real. Crap, double...no, triple negative
there. Sorry.

Anyway, I think that the people who will be most against those that make 'wild' claims like
that are going to be the ones that are in religions that do not promote those kind of
abilities. Or at least they don't in 'normal' humans. Just in those that they deem saints or
messiahs.

For a good portion of the human race, I believe that they will look at his experiences and
say, "Wow, that's very interesting." It's the people in government that have worked
extremely hard at making those qualities into fantasy and cookoo land abilities. Could be
that they are trying to hide their secrets?

I believe that with disclosure comes a lot more baggage than the governments of the
world really wanted to give up. I believe that psychic abilities and the way that THEY have
used these psychics is something that they have wanted to keep up close wraps. With
disclosure I believe that this stuff will have to come out too.
There's going to be a lot of butt kissing and long winded apologies being made I fear.

On second thought, they never apologize for anything they lie about now so I guess we
shouldn't expect one.

Gordan Cooper even went to the UN and spoke of it... Unfortunately he is no longer with
us...

    
    Astronaut Gordon Cooper's Message to the UN
  "I believe that these extraterrestrial vehicles and their crews are visiting this
  planet from other planets, which are a little more technically advanced than we
  are on Earth. I feel that we need to have a top level, coordinated program to
  scientifically collect and analyze data from all over the Earth concerning any type
  of encounter, and to determine how best to interfere with these visitors in a
  friendly fashion.

  We may first have to show them that we have learned how to resolve our
  problems by peaceful means rather than warfare, before we are accepted as
  fully qualified universal team members. Their acceptance will have tremendous
  possibilities of advancing our world in all areas. Certainly then it would seem that
  the U.N. has a vested interest in handling the subject quickly and properly.

  I should point out that I am not an experienced UFO professional researcher - I
  have not as yet had the privilege of flying a UFO nor of meeting the crew of one.
  However, I do feel that I am somewhat qualified to discuss them, since I have
  been into the fringes of the vast areas of which they travel. Also, I did have
  occasion in 1951 to have two days of observation of many flights of them, of
  different sizes flying in fighter formation, generally from west to east over
  Europe. They were at a higher altitude than we could reach with our jet
  fighters....

  If the U.N. agrees to pursue this project and lend the credibility to it, perhaps
  many more well qualified people will agree to step forth and provide help and
  information."

  [Astronaut Gordon Cooper addressing a U.N. panel discussion on UFOs and
  ETs in New York, in 1985; Panel was chaired by then U.N. Secretary-General
  Kurt Waldheim.



The above message was given to the U.N. by Astronaut Gordon L. Cooper, one of
America's original seven Mercury Astronauts. Cooper orbited the Earth for a record 34
hour, 22 orbit flight in the spacecraft 'Faith 7', in May of 1963. He has been outspoken
about the need for an open inquiry into UFOs - based on his own personal experience of
sighting UFOs in space and the testimony of other Mercury, Gemini and Apollo Astronauts.

Source: Nov. 1988 issue (Vol 1, No. 3) issue of UFO Universe magazine; Condor Books 351
West 54th St., New York, N.Y. 10019]

  Why has the government kept its UFO secrets for so many years?

  "It started in World War 2, when the government didn't want people to know
  about UFO reports in case they panicked," said Cooper. "They would have been
  fearful it was superior enemy technology that we had no defense against.

  "Then it got worse in the Cold War for the same reason.

  "So they told one untruth, they had to tell another to cover that one, then another,
  "So they told one untruth, they had to tell another to cover that one, then another,
  then another...it just snowballed.

  "And right now I'm convinced a lot of very embarrassed government officials are
  sitting there in Washington trying to figure a way to bring the truth out. They know
  it's got to come out one day, and I'm sure it will.

  "America has a right to know!" - Gordon Cooper

Seems I got my predictions of yesterday 100% right. Number 2 is even happening on this
thread, with several people already hinting (if not outright saying) that Mitchell is a bit
weird, flaky, nuts, etc... The post above me even extends that brush to astronauts in
general.

That this is 'old news' is irrelevant ... It might be so to us, but to most of the millions of
rockheads who listen to Kerrang radio I'm sure it's not.

That Mitchell has had 'faith-based' healing for cancer and thinks it worked is irrelevant.
Makes absolutely no difference to the validity (or otherwise) of his story and revelations.

That he has presented any evidence in the form of tape-recordings, photos. etc, is
irrelevant. Someone of his training, achievements, and technical background is about as
unimpeachable a witness as you can get to confirm or deny the truth of the situation.

If he had denied it all there would now be a horde of debunkers on here screaming 'SEE,
WE TOLD YOU!... You got it straight from the horse's mouth!'... The fact that he's done,
and continues to do the opposite and testify to the truth of UFOs, Aliens, and the world's
governments' involvement with them must be a real ball of bile stuck in the back of those
debunkers' throats.

That he has been doing this for ten years with little success is irrelevant. WE are now in an
age where information travels to the other side of the planet in an instance. If someone
sees a UFO in Outer Mongolia the rest of the world can know about it in a matter of
minutes. If a UFO flap occurs in the UK, the rest of the UK and the rest of the internet
connected world can know about before the MSM and TPTB have even picked up the news.

What I'm getting at is that it is becoming harder and harder to keep the lid on this thing.
Two much info immediately available to all and sundry, and sooner or later the lid will
come flying of... Mr Mitchell's continual assertions as to what he knows and has seen can
only help the cause!

Here is the one problem I have. In the UK, there is a massive tabloid underworld that
doesn't live so under the world. It has been understated for a long time now that in the UK
tabloids rule, far greater than anything offered up even here in Los Angeles. Just happens
that most of the people the world wants to see are the stars here in the states. For the
most part...

So where does that leave me when it comes to all those crop circles that pop up all over
the UK? B.S. Garbage. No proof and no nugget of truth.

The radio shows are FAR superior to anything we have in the US. It's all far understated
just how good of entertainers those Brits are. And Irish and Scottish, etc. They are
brilliant!
So when they make claims like "this will be all over the news tomorrow," and "this is a
world exclusive!!" it is transparent. As the Cranberries sang "Zombie, zombie, zombie,
zombie!!!" I sing this every time one of these stories comes through. It's NOT a 1st of
anything at all.

Give me a break tabloid UK!

All that stated - I WANT to believe these UFOs discussed are no joke, and I do believe in
Mitchell's reports having at least some merit.

hands and from other living things, so yeah, I do not consider it in any way outlandish. I
have no idea atm what to do with said energy, but I am fully aware there is more to us
than meets the average eye. I have commented on this already on a previous post in this
thread.

It's not my fault you haven't meditated and tuned your body enough to be aware of it's
potentials.

I am also aware of the benefits of something as simple as sound therapy. See: Mood.
There is more things to physics than most people are aware of, and it's silly to be closed
minded. I know there is a relation between energy and health.

BUT: even if all that energy healing stuff does not pan out it does not change the validity
of this man's statement on UFO's and coverups. A person can be right about one topic and
not about another, which is why I dislike ad hominem attacks which usually just show the
inability of the attacker to keep up with the discussed topic

Y'know...I've been a lurker here for a long time, simply observing and reading posts and
replies to subjects that interest me, and the one thing that stands out more than ALL THE
REST is that there are a few people on here who are extremely mean spirited, always
ready to jeer, poke fun at and debunk EVERYTHING that passes through...to the point of
making people just want to throw their hands up and walk away in disgust. That is not
constructive discussion friends, it's destructive discussion and criticism that should have no
place here where obviously so many intelligent, educated, and well researched
knowledgeable individuals come to talk about things outside the normal realm of
conversation everywhere else. I've learned a lot from most of you and learned a few things
I wish I didn't know from a few of you.
I understand and have seen the hoaxers first hand post on here and those of you who
debunked them and proved them to be hoaxers in a completely credible and scientifically
logical manner deserve much applause. Those who scream "foul" from the beginning and
never offer proof other than slander and "gut feelings" without any research whatsoever
are not being conducive to those here with higher learning and I've seen them cut their
noses off to spite their faces, always looking for the"angle" when sometimes, and quite
possibly in Dr.Mitchell's case, it's just the truth.
At least until someone from the medical field with AUTHORITY announces that Dr. Mitchell
is Delusional consider the possibility that he might be telling the truth.
Some of the greatest discoveries and gains in human knowledge all started with "what
if?"....
And remember that the absence of evidence does NOT necessarily mean the evidence of
absence.

Militarily Critical Technologies List (MCTL)

The Militarily Critical Technologies List (MCTL) is a compendium of existing goods and
technologies that DOD assesses would permit significant advances in the development,
production and use of military capabilities of potential adversaries.

www.dtic.mil...
Developing Science and Technologies List (DSTL)

The Developing Science and Technologies List (DSTL) is a compendium of scientific and
technological capabilities being developed worldwide that have the potential to significantly
enhance or degrade US military capabilities in the future. It includes basic research,
applied research and advanced technology development.
www.dtic.mil...

Tuesday, August 28, 2007
French COMETA UFO Report

    
    Mitchell, the Apollo 14 astronaut who was the sixth man to walk on the moon, is
    one of many supporters of international cooperation on UFOs. Of the French
    report, he says, ''It's significant that individuals of some standing in the
    government, military, and intelligence community in France came forth with this.''

    Mitchell, who holds a doctorate from MIT in aeronautics and astronautics, is
    convinced ''at a confidence level above 90 percent, that there is reality to all of
    this.'' He says, ''People have been digging through the files and investigating for
    years now. The files are quite convincing. The only thing that's lacking is the
    official stamp.''

    Mitchell joins five-star Admiral Lord Hill-Norton, the former head of the British
    Ministry of Defense, in calling for congressional fact-finding hearings into the
    UFO question.

CNN LARRY KING LIVE
Roswell Truth Debated
Aired July 4, 2008 - 21:00 ET
CNN - TRANSCRIPTS/08/07/04

(END AUDIO CLIP)

KING: We are now joined by distinguished American in West Palm Beach Dr. Edgar
Mitchell, the Apollo XIV astronaut, sixth man to walk on the moon, many of the Roswell
witnesses told Dr. Mitchell their accounts which he says were later verified by a contact at
the Pentagon. What do you believe, Dr. Mitchell?

DR. EDGAR MITCHELL, APOLLO ASTRONAUT: Well, I can say, Larry, I have no firsthand
experience but all of my experience comes from what I call the old timers that at some
point in their - before they passed on, and these folks are all gone now, and because I was
who I was, because I lived in the area, I grew up in the area, and because I was an
astronaut, some of them wanted to get it off their chest before they passed on and I
happened to be selected to hear their story. And all of them as I heard some of the earlier
testimony, all of them were under very severe oaths and fear and penalty for talking but
they did want to talk and it was ...

KING: What do you think, Dr. Mitchell, what do you think of Bill Nye's assessment when it
comes to what happened, that this was a spy balloon.

MITCHELL: I'm sorry, Bill Nye -- he was saying everybody is colored by their beliefs and not
looking at the evidence. I'm sorry, he's not either. And I think he's totally wrong ...

REST OF TRANSCRIPT

The Roswell crash was real.
Not all UFOs are of ET origin. Some are home-grown. But some are ET crafts.

We did go to the moon but there are no structures

Jim Sparks - the reptilians are good (coincides with ‘Alien intent is not hostile’)
Gordon Novel- Dulce is a disinformation operation – hence the evil reptilians (coincides
with ‘Alien intent is not hostile’)
Ralph Ring - in 1947 we already had alien technology reverse engineered (coincides with
‘Some are home-grown)

These people seem to reveal info that goes againt what Mitchell believes
Charles Hall – greys are bad (goes against ‘Alien intent is not hostile’)
Bill Holden - supports the meeting between the aliens and president Eisenhower (meaning
the story where the evil greys took over Dulce base)
Alex Christopher - believes in reptilian eating children (goes against ‘Alien intent is not
hostile’)
Dr. Greer - believes in Dulce base (goes against ‘Alien intent is not hostile’ – if you go
along with the story that Dulce Base is real, the greys and reptilians must be bad)
John Lear - the reptilians and greys are hostile (goes against ‘Alien intent is not hostile’)
Also Phil Schneider - the greys are bad and supports Dulce Base (goes against ‘Alien
intent is not hostile’)

So according to Edgar Mitchell’s info, Charles Hall, Bill Holden, Alex Christopher, Dr. Greer,
John Lear and Phil Schneider are disinfo - Phil Schneider ‘was’ disinfo, now dead.

what he's saying. Are we supposed to believe him because of his unknown contacts, who
might also be wrong, or lying to him? Or of some film he says he's seen that might or

behalf of the U.S. Government. He's not the President or Pope, who might have some bit
of believability granted to them because of their position. He's just a guy, like us, who has
done some unusual things. Haven't we all?

I've had an active interest in this UFO stuff for over 40 years. That gives me a little bit of
expertise. But even so, if I wanted to prove something, I'd still have to set it down on the
table and let everybody get a good look at it. And so does he. Nobody gets a free ride
here, because even if he believes he is telling the truth, we don't know if he actually
knows the truth until it's confirmed by those who told him in the first place.

In a recent thread (now mostly dormant) “ATS Team In Roswell, NM”, former Sgt. Clifford
Stone provided access to documents he had collected over the years, based on FOIA
requests, to Springer and Crackeur. These documents apparently show a “paper trail”
proving the existence and possession of an “artifact” determined by government officials
to be “not of Earth”.

Based on details of the artifact, as described in the documents, and the provenance of the
FOIA documents as Mr. Stone avails, Springer and Crackeur are convinced of the
authenticity of the documents, and conclude that (at least some) “UFO’s are from outer
space” is, in fact, a valid statement, acknowledged, though not publicly, by the US
government.


The “ATS Roswell Team” then began a process whereby this data, and the conclusions
drawn from said data, could be made public via the MSM. This effort is continuing.


Meanwhile, MUFON has released its findings regarding the recent UFO sightings in
Stephensville, TX. They conclude that the reports of eyewitnesses in Stephensville of UFO’s
appearing during January 2008 are supported by radar return data made available to
MUFON via FOIA requests.

MUFON also concludes that at least one UFO witnessed during this episode flew in a
direction that would have allowed it to encroach upon the restricted airspace above the US
President’s ranch in Crawford, TX: and, in fact the object’s last radar return showed it
President’s ranch in Crawford, TX: and, in fact the object’s last radar return showed it
approximately 10 miles from the restricted zone before it disappeared from the radar.

Disturbingly, military and security forces, however, took no apparent action to either
positively identify or intercept this obvious and potential threat.


It should be noted that this curious “Lack of Response” on the part of military and/or
security forces is similar in character to an incident also involving a UFO in 2007. That
incident involved the incursion of an unresponsive UFO into the restricted airspace over
O’Hare International airport. Despite “hovering” over the airport for 30-45 minutes, there
is no report of officials attempting to identify or intercept the object; despite the obvious
threat to public safety is presence in that location posed.



Now we have former astronaut Dr. Edgar Mitchell, on record, claiming that he believes
that the famous incident in Roswell, NM involved the crash and recovery of a UFO from
outer space. He also asserts that at least some UFO’s are not from Earth, and that the US
government, as well as other governments of the world, know that “UFO’s are Real”,
have known this for decades, and have been keeping this knowledge from the public.
Convergence?

Both Dr. Mitchell and Sgt. Stone hail from Roswell, NM. Dr Mitchell grew up in Roswell; Sgt.
Stone currently lives in Roswell.


In researching the “Stone Documents”, Crackeur and Springer requested the assistance of
ATS members in determining the maximum payload for launch vehicles available during the
‘60’s and ‘70’s. Springer also mentioned that the documents they were reviewing showed
that gov’t officials, having determined that an object in their possession was, in fact,
extra-terrestrial in origin, then documented the shipment of the object to a location in
the US; whereupon no further trace of the object can be located.

If the source of Springer’s “Maximum Payload” query and the “lost” artifact are one in
the same, might the object in question have been too large to ship by aircraft?

That would mean that the object would have had to been handled by either ground
transport or by sea.

An object too large to be shipped by aircraft would have likely had many witnesses when it
landed (crashed?) if it were to have done so anywhere within the US/Canada. Its “arrival”
would likely have been the source of media coverage and speculation. But a very large
object landing/crashing outside the US/Canada confines would be difficult and impractical
to trans-ship by ground vehicle.

If the object in question was recovered from somewhere other than the US/ Canada, the
most logical means of returning it to the US for further study would be by sea: US Navy,
perhaps?

One of the incident Sgt. Stone claims to have been dispatched to involved a crashed US B-
52 bomber in Vietnam that he said, “looked like it had been placed on the ground” in the
jungle “by a large hand”.


Question: Could this incident have actually been a UFO recovery operation – “Masked” as
an airplane crash memory?


Dr. Mitchell’s assertions regarding the reality of UFO’s arise not just from his faith in the
“old timers” he’s spoken to in Roswell, but also from other sources he’s been privy to: He
specifically mentions the influence an interview he had with an Admiral in the Pentagon.


If a “B-52 -sized” UFO crashed in Vietnam, was recovered by US forces, and shipped back
to the US for further study by the US Navy, who better to know about it than an admiral
in the Pentagon?



The possible relationship between/among Sgt. Clifford Stone and his FOIA documents, the
The possible relationship between/among Sgt. Clifford Stone and his FOIA documents, the
“ATS Roswell Team”, the incidents at O’Hare and Stephensville, and now Dr. Edgar
Mitchell’s assertions is intriguing to say the least.



Is AboveTopSecret playing a bigger role in “Disclosure” than they have been letting on?

A much bigger role than any of us have, or could possibly, imagine?

reply to post by Tricky63

I sincerely doubt we will be hearing anything from the government on this anytime soon. If
aliens are visiting our planet and have no hostile intent, why don't they show themselves? If
they're visiting as has been alleged, spying on us without making direct contact, I wouldn't
trust them.

If aliens are truly visiting, they obviously have technologies that we haven't even dreamed
of. The aliens would have some means of travel that we can barely theorize about, much
less successfully engineer. In short, there's nothing we can do about them. We are almost
completely defenseless. What benefit is it for the government to cause a mass panic? It
serves no useful purpose until we can devise effective counter measures against these
alien intruders.

Then, with defensive technologies in hand, if they try to come here, we should fend them
off and only allow them to approach on OUR terms. The Japanese successfully assimilated
western technologies and became a modern nation state in one generation. Japan should
be our "template." if these aliens truly are visiting here. We could assimilate their
technologies but maintain our own identity as Japan has done.

The other "template" could be the native Americans who by and large failed to assimilate
European technologies sufficient to defend themselves against the white man's
encroachments. The Cherokees very nearly brought it off but did not acquire the requisite
military power to hold onto their lands in Georgia. They trusted the good faith of the
white man. Big mistake. We should be suspicious of the "gaijin" (foreign devils) like the
Japanese, not trusting like the Cherokees. The aliens might not like that, but at least they
would learn to respect us.

reply to post by zorgon
I thought everyone had heard of Drudge...I guess I would describe it as basically the pulse
of what is happening in the world at any given point in time on a very Google like site
(mostly text, no obnoxious distractions, etc..)

Normally it just has links to existing news stories, however they sometimes do their own
stories.

When stories break (from any news source), you can be assured it will be visible on
Drudge, and shortly thereafter, known to the rest of the media and world.

It is very influential on setting media agenda--if something appears on there, it will
become a topic of further discussion.

Take a look at Wikipedia--there is a very good write up on the stats, history, etc...

delius

rocksarerocks
posted on Jul, 30 2008 @ 10:47 AM                                                         link

I posted this in another thread, it really needs to go here. I agree with badastronomy.
I posted this in another thread, it really needs to go here. I agree with badastronomy.
___________________________
Apollo astronauts were heroes. That is very clear to me and should be to everyone; they
took an incredible risk to explore and further the knowledge of mankind.

However, that does not give them a "get out of reality free" card.

Ed Mitchell walked on the Moon for the Apollo 14 mission. OK, got that? He walked on the
Moon. I have no issue with that.

However, he also believes aliens exist. That’s fine too; in fact, while wouldn’t use the
word "believe", I strongly suspect there are aliens elsewhere in the Universe as well. Where
Mitchell and I part ways is that he thinks that the aliens are and have been coming here,
the government knows, and has been covering it up for decades.

Thing is, his feelings on this have been pretty clear for a while now. But he recently stated
them on the radio, which has led to wide reporting on the topic, like say here. There are
a zillion other sources if you care (roughly the same number of emails I’ve gotten on this
topic).

OK, yes, an Apollo Moonwalker thinks greys are coming here and probing us or whatever.
Fine. He can believe what he wants. I think he’s wrong, but he has the right to believe
that.

Two points on this. First, of course, the UFO folks will go ballistic over this, because, after
all, Mitchell was an astronaut and walked on the Moon. But then, see my "get out of
reality card" comment above. Also, this is simply argument from authority, which is bad
logic. It doesn’t matter who believes in aliens. What matters is — hello! — evidence.

Show me the clear pictures. She me to nonterrestrial-isotope-laden artifacts. Show me a
frakkin’ flying saucer sitting on the White House lawn.

Show me anything other than blurry pictures and videos, eyewitness testimony, and black-
out government documents. I want evidence.

The second point is Mitchell’s history. He has long been an advocate for what can be called
at best fringe science, starting with his tests of psychic powers on that same lunar flight.
That’s fine — in fact I support things like that, if done rigorously — but clearly in my
opinion he goes way too far. Government coverups of actual aliens? Really? Where’s the
proof of that? The evidence is always circumstantial, and that makes me suspicious
indeed. We have governments that cannot coverup a simple break in, a tryst in the White
House, or trade deals between Iran and the contras. Those are cakewalks compared to the
massive conspiracy theorized by the UFO crowd.

My point is not to make an ad hominem attack Mitchell’s credibility; he is a nice man and
a gentleman. I met him and he was very kind to me, and he told me he literally kicked
Bart Sibrel out of his house, which makes my heart sing.

No, my point is that I don’t really see why this is suddenly news. He’s been talking fringe
science for decades.

So I want to be very clear here: I have enormous, deep, and profound respect for all the
men who walked on the Moon, and all the men and women who put them there. But that
does not give them a pass to ignore evidence and to draw drastically unrealistic conclusions
without evidence to support them.


Source:
blogs.discovermagazine.com...
